Let me design the auto-size algorithm ("Reader Fit"):

text

function autoFontSize(w, h) {
  // Safari reader ~ uses 17pt on phone; scale mildly with width, respect height
  const base = clamp(17, 15 + w * 0.012, 24); // e.g. 390px → 19.7, 1440 → 24 (capped)
  // also consider measure: we want ~60-75 chars per line; with max width 34em cap
}

EPUB structure details:

container.xml: points to OEBPS/content.opf
content.opf: package with metadata (dc:title, dc:creator, dc:identifier, dc:language, meta dcterms:modified), manifest (items: nav.xhtml properties="nav", ncx, chapters, css), spine.
toc.ncx for compatibility; epub.js uses nav.xhtml if present in EPUB3.
CSS inside epub minimal since we override with themes anyway, but include a bit (headings, blockquote).
Writing the zip with JSZip: zip.generateAsync({type:"arraybuffer", mimeType:"application/epub+zip"}) then ePub(buffer).

Bionic processing:

text

function bionify(doc){
  const walker = doc.createTreeWalker(doc.body, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT, {
    acceptNode: n => {
      if(!n.nodeValue.trim()) return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;
      const p = n.parentElement; if(!p) return REJECT;
      const tag = p.tagName;
      if(/^(SCRIPT|STYLE|TITLE|HEAD)$/.test(tag)) return REJECT;
      // skip already inside .b-w span
      if(p.closest && p.closest('.b-w')) return REJECT;
      return FILTER_ACCEPT;
    }});
  const nodes=[]; while(walker.nextNode()) nodes.push(walker.currentNode);
  nodes.forEach(n=>{ 
    const frag = doc.createDocumentFragment();
    n.nodeValue.split(/(\s+)/).forEach(tok=>{
      if(!tok) return;
      if(/^\s+$/.test(tok)){ frag.appendChild(doc.createTextNode(tok)); return; }
      const letters = tok.replace(/[^A-Za-z\u00C0-\u024F\u0370-\u03FF\u0400-\u04FF]/g,'');  // count letters
      const lm = tok.match(/^[\P{L}]*([\p{L}]+)/u); 
      ... 
    });
    n.parentNode.replaceChild(frag, n);
  });
}

Keep it simple: len>=2: boldCount = Math.max(1, Math.round(len*0.42)); wrap prefix in <strong class="b-w">? Title page:

text

THE TIME MACHINE
by H. G. Wells
An abridgement in three chapters, set by Folio as a working sample.
London · 1895
Ch I — The Fourth Dimension:
"The Time Traveller (for so it will be convenient to speak of him) was expounding a recondite matter to us. His grey eyes shone and twinkled, and his usually pale face was flushed and animated. The fire burned brightly, and the soft radiance of the incandescent lights in the lilies of silver caught the bubbles that flashed and passed in our glasses.

Our chairs, being his patents, embraced and caressed us rather than submitted to be sat upon...

'The Time Traveller proceeded: '...You must follow me carefully. I shall have to dispute one or two points that are almost universally accepted. The geometry, for instance, which they have taught you at school is founded on a misconception.'

'Is not that rather a large thing to expect us to begin upon?' said Filby.

'I do not mean to ask you to accept anything without reasonable ground for belief...'

'Clearly,' the Time Traveller proceeded, 'any real body must have extension in four directions: it must have Length, Breadth, Thickness, and—Duration... There are really four dimensions, three which we call the three planes of Space, and a fourth, Time. There is, however, a tendency to draw an unreal distinction between the former three dimensions and the latter, because it happens that our consciousness moves intermittently in one direction along the latter from the beginning of our existence to the end.'

... quote about scientific people: 'Some philosophical people have been asking why three dimensions particularly—why not another direction at right angles to the other three?—and have even tried to construct a Four-Dimension geometry. Professor Simon Newcomb was expounding this to the New York Mathematical Society only a month or so ago...'

end: 'That,' said a very young man, 'is splendid science.' / 'In spite of it all, we shall see the thing work tonight,' someone murmured..." I'll compose coherent abridged paragraphs. It's fine as abridgement — I'll note "abridged".

Ch II — The Model Machine:
famous: "The Time Traveller gave us a demonstration... a little mechanism of glittering metal, scarcely larger than a small clock, very delicately made... 'This little thing,' he said, 'will travel either into the future or into the past...' He pointed to the lever... 'Press this lever in the other direction, and you will send it into the future. Actually it will vanish from our view...' The Psychologist... pressed the lever... We looked at the empty socket... 'In a moment,' said the Time Traveller... The Psychologist recovered from his stupor, and suddenly looked under the table... 'Are you sure? Is it not an hallucination?'... 'That is all a fraud... the thing disappeared?' Filby? Famous exchange with the Medical Man about the little thing in the smoke... 'the story of the vanishing machine'... Then: '...the full-sized machine in the laboratory... Thursday for another dinner. We came again the following week and the machine stood, huge saddle-like... The Time Traveller mounted... pressed the lever...' Actually the disappearance of TT happens end of ch 3? In the frame: ch 2 ends with the model demo; the big machine ride is revealed later (ch 3 narrator realizes TT gone). For abridgement, Ch II can end with the model demonstration + plans for Thursday.

Ch III — The Sensation of a Leap: TT's own account (told to the second dinner): "I told you all last Thursday... You see I have been in the future... I started the machine... the odd sensation of falling... 'I am afraid I cannot convey the peculiar sensations of the time traveller... a kind of headlong hurry... night came like the flipping of a black switch... tomorrow... faster... the great buildings vanished... the sun spanning the sky... I saw great and splendid architecture rising about me... a huge butterfly... the Palacaeur? '...the slowest snail-paced clock was trickling...' famous lines: 'The peculiar risk lay in the possibility of my finding some creature of my own... acutely ?' Keep ~5 paragraphs ending mid-adventure with: '...And I have by me, for my comfort, two strange white flowers—shrunken, brown, and soft and brittle... shut out forever from the little world of the Eloi? ...' Actually those flowers are end of book. End chapter: '...the dim radiance of the spreading dawn... I resolved to stop.' Then narrator's closing line: 'Here the Time Traveller paused and put the machine?' I'll end with narrator: "There was a pause. The Medical Man took up a lamp..."? For a sample ending: "He paused, looked into the fire, and went on." Fine.

I'll write it well but compact. Each chapter 5-7 paragraphs.

TTS engine:

text

const tts = {on:false, paused:false, token:0, chain:false};
async function ttsStart(){
  if(!window.speechSynthesis){toast('Speech not supported');return}
  if(tts.on){ // toggle pause
    if(speechSynthesis.paused){speechSynthesis.resume(); tts.paused=false}
    else {speechSynthesis.pause(); tts.paused=true}
    updateSpeakBtn(); return;
  }
  tts.on=true; tts.chain=true; const my=++tts.token; updateSpeakBtn();
  loadVoices? ensure voices; 
  while(tts.on && tts.token===my){
    const pageText = await currentPageText(); if(!pageText){break}
    const sents = splitSentences(pageText);
    markPage(); // annotation highlight current page
    for(const s of sents){ if(tts.token!==my||!tts.on) break;
      await speak(s, my);
    }
    unmarkPage();
    if(tts.token!==my||!tts.on) break;
    // next page
    const atEnd = ...; if end → toast('End of book') break;
    tts.pendingNext = true; state? rendition.next();
    await waitForRelocated(); // promise resolved by relocated handler if pendingNext... implement: await new Promise(res=>{ const h=()=>{rendition.off('relocated',h);res()}; rendition.on('relocated',h); });
  }
  if(tts.token===my){tts.on=false;updateSpeakBtn()}
}
function ttsStop(){tts.token++;tts.on=false;tts.paused=false;speechSynthesis.cancel();unmarkPage();updateSpeakBtn();}
speak(text, my){return new Promise(res=>{ const u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text); u.rate=settings.rate; u.voice=chosenVoice; u.onend=res; u.onerror=res; speechSynthesis.speak(u); // watchdog: if synth silently fails (no voices), resolve via timer? add safety: if speechSynthesis.speaking false after 300ms & no onstart → res? keep simple; onstart flag with 1.5s check.
})}
currentPageText(){ const loc = rendition.currentLocation(); if(!loc.start) return ''; try{ const range = await book.getRange(loc.start.cfi+','+loc.end.cfi); return range.toString(); }catch{ fallback: read from section? return '' }
}
Wait — currentLocation().end may equal start on last page w/ short? fine.

End of book: if loc.end at last section end → loc.atEnd? epub location has 'atEnd' boolean? IIRC location object may not; check loc.end.index === book.spine last index and loc.end.percentage>0.999? Use loc.start.percentage >= 0.999 → end. OK.

markPage: annotations.add('tts', range cfiRange... need cfiRange string same; annotations.add(type, cfiRange, data, cb, className, styles) — signature per docs: add(type, cfiRange, data, cb, className, styles). ✓ remove: annotations.remove(cfiRange, 'tts').

Note getRange returns a Range (DOM) whose toString gives text ✓.

Guard: some epubs produce weird CFI ranges mid-page — try/catch → toast once then abort TTS.

Debionify:

text

function debionify(doc){ doc.querySelectorAll('b.fio-b').forEach(b=>{ const t=doc.createTextNode(b.textContent); b.replaceWith(t); }); doc.normalize(); }

css

@font-face ×2 OpenDyslexic;
html{ -webkit-text-size-adjust:100%; }
html,body{ background:${page} !important; }
body{
  color:${ink} !important;
  font-family:${stack} !important;
  font-size:${fs}px !important;
  line-height:${lh} !important;
  letter-spacing:${ls}em !important;
  word-spacing:${ws}em !important;
  text-align:${justify?'justify':'left'} !important;
  hyphens:auto; -webkit-hyphens:auto; overflow-wrap:break-word;
  padding: 0 !important;  /* control via stage */
  margin: 0 !important;
}

Demo creation function:

js

async function makeDemo(){
 const zip = new JSZip();
 zip.file('mimetype','application/epub+zip',{compression:'STORE'});
 zip.file('META-INF/container.xml', `<?xml version="1.0"?>...`);
 const css = `body{font-family:serif}h2{...}` minor;

Chapters text — write now (concise but quality):

CH1 "The Fourth Dimension" (~7 paragraphs):

P1: “The Time Traveller (for so it will be convenient to speak of him) was expounding a recondite matter to us. His grey eyes shone and twinkled, and his usually pale face was flushed and animated. The fire burned brightly, and the soft radiance of the incandescent lights in the lilies of silver caught the bubbles that flashed and passed in our glasses.”

P2: “Our chairs, being his patents, embraced and caressed us rather than submitted to be sat upon; and there was that luxurious after-dinner atmosphere, when thought runs gracefully free of the trammels of precision. And slowly and patiently the Time Traveller laid his before us.”

P3: “‘You must follow me carefully. I shall have to dispute one or two points that are almost universally accepted. The geometry, for instance, which they have taught you at school is founded on a misconception.’ ‘Is not that rather a large thing to expect us to begin upon?’ said Filby, an argumentative person with red hair. ‘I do not mean to ask you to accept anything without reasonable ground for belief. Soon you will easily grant me the point I ask, and return to your a priori ideas again.’” — hmm mixing quotes w/o attribution formatting fine.

P4 blockquote: “‘Clearly,’ the Time Traveller proceeded, ‘any real body must have extension in four directions: it must have Length, Breadth, Thickness, and Duration. There are really four dimensions, three which we call the three planes of Space, and a fourth, Time. There is, however, a tendency to draw an unreal distinction between the former three dimensions and the latter, because it happens that our consciousness moves intermittently in one direction along the latter from the beginning of our existence to the end.’” → blockquote styling demo. Use <blockquote><p>…</p></blockquote>.

P5: “‘But,’ said the Medical Man, staring hard at a poker in the fire, ‘a thing cannot be in different parts of Space at the same moment, since it must occupy the same Space at every moment.’ ‘Scientific people,’ the Time Traveller remarked, with a glance at the fire, ‘know very well that Time is only a kind of Space. Here is a portrait of a man at eight years old, yet another at fifteen, another at seventeen, another at twenty-three, and so on. All these are evidently sections, as it were, Three-Dimensional representations of his Four-Dimensioned being, which is a fixed and unalterable thing.’” — I'm loosely reproducing; PD fine.

P6: “There was a murmur of dissent. Filby pronounced the notion ‘fanciful and unbusinesslike’, and the very young man demanded, above the clink of glasses, why one should not move about in Time as freely as one moved about in Space. The Time Traveller only smiled, and tapped his forefinger on the arm of his chair.”

P7: “‘That,’ he said, ‘is the beginning. Tonight I will show you a machine — and if it does what I believe it can do, none of us will sleep very soundly again.’ ”

CH2 “The Model Machine”:

P1: “The next Thursday we came again — the Psychologist, the Medical Man, Filby, the Very Young Man, and myself. We found the Time Traveller in the drawing-room, a little battered and dusty about the elbows, and a little hollow in the cheek. ‘I’m dying to show you something,’ he said, and led us down the long draughty corridor to the laboratory.”

P2: “On the table lay a little mechanism of glittering metal, scarcely larger than a small clock, very delicately made. There was ivory in it, and some transparent crystalline substance. ‘This little thing,’ said the Time Traveller, resting his elbows on the table and pressing his hands together above it, ‘is a model of a machine that will travel through Time.’ ”

P3: “‘This lever here,’ he went on, ‘sends the thing gliding into the future. This other sends it into the past. I will press one of them, and the little machine will vanish — from this room, from this moment, into whatever lies ahead of it.’ The Psychologist, at a sign from our host, put out his finger and pressed the gleaming lever.”

P4: “There was a breath of wind, a flicker of the lamplight, a little turning of the flames in the grate — and where the model had stood there was nothing at all. We looked at the bare table. Somebody gasped. The Psychologist recovered from his stupor, and suddenly looked under the table.”

P5: “‘Are you in earnest?’ said the Medical Man. ‘Is it a trick — a conjuring trick?’ ‘That,’ said the Time Traveller, ‘is not a trick. The little machine is at this moment travelling onward, year by year, in the middle of that room. Watch the smoke: it is drifting through the space the model has left behind it, and through the centuries too.’ ” hmm invented-ish flavor but in spirit. PD original has Medical Man fumbling lighter & the smoke line is about the little thing going through time among smoke. Fine for abridgement.

P6: “We stood around the empty place a long time, no one speaking. Outside, the evening deepened; and I remember thinking that somewhere down the far slope of Time that small bright thing was still falling, and would be falling still when every one of us was gone.”

P7: “‘The full-sized machine,’ said the Time Traveller at last, blowing out the lamp, ‘stands in the laboratory. Come back on Thursday — and bring your nerve with you.’ ”

CH3 “The Sensation of a Leap”: Frame: next Thursday, TT appears dusty & lame telling his tale.

P1: “When we came again the next Thursday, the laboratory stood empty. The Time Traveller was not there. And then, in the doorway, haggard and pale, with a dirty bandage on his ankle and his clothes dusty and disordered, he came in — the man himself, returned from wherever the machine had taken him.”

Hmm actually in the original, the narrator realizes at dinner (ch 3?) that TT had travelled during their previous? The frame: The TT tells the story a week after his departure; the dinner where he narrates. My compression acceptable for the sample.

P2: “‘I started the machine that morning,’ he said, when we had drawn up to the fire. ‘I pressed the lever for the future. And I am afraid I cannot convey the peculiar sensations of travelling through Time. It is a headlong fall, a ghastly flipping of days and nights — the laboratory grew misty, Mrs. Watchett crossed the room like a rocket, and then the night came like the shutting of a black lid.’” — mixing famous lines: "I saw the laboratory... Mrs. Watchett ... like a rocket" ✓ original-ish.

P3 blockquote: “‘As I went on, night followed day like the flipping of a black and white page. The speeding of the clock about me grew prodigious. The dim outline of the laboratory vanished; I saw the sun spin across the sky in rings of light; I saw the sky take on a wonderful tint of dawn and dusk together, and the stars beat in and out among them. The landscape rose and fell like the tide; the trees grew and changed and passed like breath; and building after building, age after age, flashed into being and was gone.’” — flavor of original ("night came like the flipping...").

P4: “‘I was afraid to stop. And then, all at once, the change of pace told me I must. I brought the machine to rest — and the sun stood still in a sky of exquisite sunset, and before me, washed in that gentler light, lay a great and beautiful architecture of porcelain and stone, and a small, graceful people gathered to look at me, as silent as the statues they lived among.’” (Eloi arrival, compressed.)

P5: “‘Of what I found there — of the two peoples of that far afternoon of the world, the childlike Eloi above ground and the pale Morlocks below — of the dark wells, the green enamel palaces, and the fire I carried into the under-world — I will tell you everything, if you will bear with me. I am tired, and limping, and hungry; but I have by me two strange white flowers, shrunken and brown now, that grew beside the way, to remind me of that other and happier morning.’” — White flowers (Weena's flowers) — end-of-book image; used as foreshadow. It's an abridgement; acceptable, and evocative.

P6 narrator: “He paused. The fire had burnt low, and the rest of us sat very still, our glasses forgotten. Somewhere in the laboratory, faint in the draught, something ticked — and for the first time that evening, I believed him.”

function onKey(e){ if(e.defaultPrevented) return; const tag=(e.target&&e.target.tagName)||''; if(/INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA/.test(tag)) return;

 switch(e.key){ case 'ArrowRight': case 'PageDown': turn(1); e.preventDefault(); break; case ' ': if(!e.shiftKey){turn(1);e.preventDefault()} break; case 'ArrowLeft': case 'PageUp': turn(-1); e.preventDefault(); break; case 't': toggleToc(); break; case 's': toggleSheet(); break; case 'g': toggleGuide(); break; case 'b': setBionic(!settings.bionic); syncSwitch(); break; case 'p': toggleSpeak(); break; }
}

  await closeBook();

Now fit & relayout code:

text

function fitInfo(){
 const m=settings.measure;
 const padX=Math.max(16,Math.min(innerWidth*0.05,48));
 const w=innerWidth-padX*2;
 const h=innerHeight-150;
 const fit=Math.max(17, Math.min(w/m, 700/m, h/26, 23));
 return {fs: Math.round(fit*10)/10, mw: Math.min(m*fit, w), m};
}
function effSize(){ return settings.auto? fitInfo().fs : settings.size; }
function applyFit(){
 const {fs,mw}=fitInfo();
 const es=effSize();
 document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--fit-fs', es+'px');
 document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--fit-lh', settings.lineHeight);
 const stage=$('#stage'); stage.style.setProperty('--mw', Math.round(settings.measure*es)+'px');
 sizeRange value sync; sizeOut text: (settings.auto? fs.toFixed(1): settings.size.toFixed(1))+'px';
 measureOut: settings.measure+'em';
 colophons: colophonEl.textContent = coloText();
 landingColophon same.
 arrows visibility: stage.classList.toggle('no-arrows', innerWidth - Math.round(settings.measure*es) < 150);
}
function relayout(){ applyFit(); refreshCSS(); if(state.rendition){ const wrap=$('#wrap'); try{ state.rendition.resize(wrap.clientWidth, wrap.clientHeight) }catch(e){} } }
--mw in stage: stage grid-template-columns uses var(--mw) ✓ but should cap at available: minmax(0, var(--mw)) with stage padding handles.

refreshCSS(): eachContent update style text; plus store for future via hook (hook uses buildCSS fresh ✓).

sizeAuto click: settings.auto=true; relayout(); save; sync chip.
lh input: settings.lineHeight=+v; refreshCSS(); outs; save; (lh affects pages count → resize? line-height changes pagination — epub.js recalcs on resize only; text reflows within columns automatically since same container; paginated layout depends on content height; iframe height fixed; line-height change reflows columns → epub.js might not notice → next/prev page boundaries stale? The iframe uses CSS columns; changing line-height reflows columns but scrollWidth changes; epub.js page detection uses overflow & column width — should still work since it computes pages from scroll width at turn time? It caches page count per section at layout. To be safe: after css-affecting-flow changes (lh, letters, word, font, justify) call debounced resize too. I'll just always relayout() (resize included) with rAF debounce on every setting change. Flicker minimal.

eachContent:

js

function eachContent(fn){
  const r = state.rendition; if(!r) return;
  let list = [];
  try { list = r.getContents ? (r.getContents()||[]) : []; } catch(e){ list = []; }
  list.forEach(fn);
}
onContent(contents):

js

function onContent(contents){
  const doc = contents.document;
  let st = doc.getElementById('folio-dyn');
  if(!st){ st = doc.createElement('style'); st.id='folio-dyn'; doc.head.appendChild(st); }
  st.textContent = buildCSS();
  if(settings.bionic && doc.body.dataset.fioBionic!=='1'){ bionifyDoc(doc); doc.body.dataset.fioBionic='1'; }
  wireContents(contents);
}
wireContents:

js

function wireContents(contents){
  const doc = contents.document;
  doc.addEventListener('click', (e)=>{
    const sel = doc.getSelection ? doc.getSelection() : null;
    if(sel && sel.toString().length) return;
    const a = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('a') : null;
    if(a) return; // let epub.js handle links
    const w = (doc.documentElement && doc.documentElement.clientWidth) || 1;
    const x = e.clientX / w;
    if(x < 0.3) turn(-1);
    else if(x > 0.7) turn(1);
    else if(matchMedia('(hover: none)').matches) toggleChrome();
  });
  doc.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
}
refreshCSS:

js

function refreshCSS(){
  const css = buildCSS();
  eachContent(c=>{
    const doc=c.document; let st=doc.getElementById('folio-dyn');
    if(!st){st=doc.createElement('style');st.id='folio-dyn';(doc.head||doc.documentElement).appendChild(st);}
    st.textContent=css;
  });
}
buildCSS():

function buildCSS(){
  const t=THEMES[settings.theme]||THEMES.paper;
  const fs=effSize();
  const stack=FONT_STACKS[settings.font]||FONT_STACKS.literata;
  const justify = settings.justify;
  const acc = t.accent;
  return `
${FONT_FACES}
html{font-size:${fs}px !important;-webkit-text-size-adjust:100%;}
html,body{background:${t.page} !important;}
body{
 color:${t.ink} !important;
 font-family:${stack} !important;
 font-size:${fs}px !important;
 line-height:${settings.lineHeight} !important;
 letter-spacing:${(settings.letter/1000).toFixed(3)}em !important;
 word-spacing:${(settings.word/1000).toFixed(3)}em !important;
 text-align:${justify?'justify':'left'} !important;
 margin:0 !important; padding:0 !important;
 -webkit-hyphens:auto; hyphens:auto; overflow-wrap:break-word;
}
p,li,dd,dt,blockquote,td,th,figcaption,div,section,article,span,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,a,strong,b,em,i,small{letter-spacing:inherit !important;word-spacing:inherit !important;line-height:inherit !important;}
p,li,dd,dt,blockquote,td,th,figcaption,div,section,article,pre{font-size:1rem !important;}
h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6{font-family:${stack} !important;color:inherit !important;text-align:${justify?'left':'inherit'} !important;}
p{margin:0 0 1.05em !important;text-indent:0 !important;orphans:2;widows:2;text-wrap:pretty;}
h1,h2,h3{margin:1.6em 0 .8em !important;}
blockquote{margin:1.3em 1.6em !important;padding:0 0 0 1.1em;border-left:2px solid ${rgba(t.hairS? use hairS)};}

Bionify:

js

function bionifyDoc(doc){
  const walker=doc.createTreeWalker(doc.body,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT,{acceptNode(n){
    if(!n.nodeValue||!n.nodeValue.trim())return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;
    const p=n.parentElement; if(!p)return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;
    const tag=p.nodeName; if(tag==='SCRIPT'||tag==='STYLE'||tag==='HEAD'||tag==='TITLE')return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;
    return NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT;
  }});
  const nodes=[]; while(walker.nextNode())nodes.push(walker.currentNode);
  const WORD=/^([\P{L}\P{N}]*)([\p{L}\p{N}]+)([\s\S]*)$/u;
  nodes.forEach(node=>{
    const frag=doc.createDocumentFragment();
    node.nodeValue.split(/(\s+)/).forEach(tok=>{
      if(!tok)return;
      const m=tok.match(WORD);
      if(!m||m[2].length<2){frag.appendChild(doc.createTextNode(tok));return;}
      const n=Math.max(1,Math.round(m[2].length*0.42));
      if(m[1])frag.appendChild(doc.createTextNode(m[1]));
      const b=doc.createElement('b');b.className='fio-b';b.textContent=m[2].slice(0,n);
      frag.appendChild(b);
      if(m[2].length>n)frag.appendChild(doc.createTextNode(m[2].slice(n)));
      if(m[3])frag.appendChild(doc.createTextNode(m[3]));
    });
    node.parentNode.replaceChild(frag,node);
  });
}
function debionify(doc){
  doc.querySelectorAll('b.fio-b').forEach(b=>{
    b.replaceWith(doc.createTextNode(b.textContent));
  });
  doc.body.normalize();
}
Regex with \P{L} leading punct — [\P{L}\P{N}] means chars not letter& not number... within class, \P{L} is a class-like escape — [\P{L}\P{N}] valid in u-mode ✓ matches punctuation/symbols. Word = letters+digits ✓. Emphasis inside words fine.

NodeFilter on huge docs ok.

TTS:

js

const tts={active:false,paused:false,token:0};
function chosenVoice(){
  if(!('speechSynthesis' in window))return null;
  const vs=speechSynthesis.getVoices(); if(!vs.length)return null;
  let v=vs.find(x=>x.voiceURI===settings.voice);
  if(!v){ const lang=(((state.book||{}).packaging||{}).metadata||{}).language||'en';
    v=vs.find(x=>x.lang&&x.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(String(lang).slice(0,2).toLowerCase())); }
  return v||vs[0];
}
function speak(text,my){return new Promise(res=>{
  const u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  const v=chosenVoice(); if(v)u.voice=v;
  u.rate=settings.rate; u.pitch=1;
  let done=false; const fin=()=>{if(!done){done=true;res();}};
  u.onend=fin; u.onerror=fin;
  speechSynthesis.speak(u);
});}
async function pageRangeText(){
  const loc=state.rendition.currentLocation();
  if(!loc||!loc.start||!loc.end)return null;
  const cr=loc.start.cfi+','+loc.end.cfi;
  try{ const range=await state.book.getRange(cr); const txt=(range&&range.toString?range.toString():'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim(); return txt?{txt,cr}:null; }
  catch(e){ return {err:true}; }
}
async function speakFromHere(){
  if(!state.rendition)return;
  if(!('speechSynthesis' in window)){toast('Speech synthesis is not available in this browser');return;}
  if(tts.active){ // toggle pause
    if(speechSynthesis.paused){speechSynthesis.resume();tts.paused=false;}
    else if(speechSynthesis.speaking){speechSynthesis.pause();tts.paused=true;}
    syncSpeakBtn(); return;
  }
  if(!speechSynthesis.getVoices().length){ // give voices a beat to load
    await new Promise(r=>{let n=0;const t=setInterval(()=>{if(speechSynthesis.getVoices().length||++n>20){clearInterval(t);r();}},100);});
  }
  tts.active=true;tts.paused=false;const my=++tts.token;syncSpeakBtn();
Wait toggleSpeak duplicates pause logic — unify: btn click → if(!state.rendition) return; if(tts.active){pause/resume logic} else speakFromHere(). Implement one handler.

Here's my take on this: a single-file reading room I'm calling **Folio**. It opens any DRM-free EPUB (or drag-and-drop it), sets the type the way Safari Reader does — size derived from the actual screen, a capped measure around 65–70 characters per line, generous leading — and ships a dyslexia toolkit (OpenDyslexic, Atkinson Hyperlegible, Lexend, bionic reading, letter/word-spacing, a tracking guide, and read-aloud with auto page turns). It comes with a built-in sample book (an abridged *Time Machine*) generated in-memory as a real EPUB so you can try it with zero files.

```html

<script>
'use strict';
/* =====================================================================
   Folio — a single-file EPUB reading room
   ===================================================================== */
const el = id => document.getElementById(id);
const clamp = (v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
const body = document.body;

/* ---------------- settings ---------------- */
const DEFAULTS = {
  theme:'paper', font:'literata', auto:true, size:19.5,
  lineHeight:1.65, letter:0, word:0, measure:34, justify:false,
  bionic:false, guide:false, rate:1, voice:null
};
let settings = {...DEFAULTS};
try{ Object.assign(settings, JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('folio-settings')||'{}')); }catch(e){}
let saveT=null;
function save(){ clearTimeout(saveT); saveT=setTimeout(()=>{ try{localStorage.setItem('folio-settings',JSON.stringify(settings));}catch(e){} },150); }

/* ---------------- themes ---------------- */
const THEMES = {
  paper:   {label:'Paper',    desk:'#e8dfca', page:'#f7f1e3', ink:'#26211a', muted:'#7a6c56', accent:'#b23c17', hair:'rgba(38,33,26,.15)',  hairS:'rgba(38,33,26,.34)', dim:'rgba(31,24,14,.14)',  sel:'rgba(178,60,23,.25)'},
  sepia:   {label:'Sepia',    desk:'#dfcfae', page:'#f1e4c8', ink:'#4a3a22', muted:'#8a7452', accent:'#8c4a18', hair:'rgba(74,58,34,.18)',  hairS:'rgba(74,58,34,.42)', dim:'rgba(60,45,20,.12)',  sel:'rgba(140,74,24,.25)'},
  noir:    {label:'Noir',     desk:'#0f0d0a', page:'#171410', ink:'#e8ddc7', muted:'#a08f74', accent:'#e0764a', hair:'rgba(232,221,199,.14)',hairS:'rgba(232,221,199,.36)',dim:'rgba(0,0,0,.4)',   sel:'rgba(224,118,74,.32)'},
  contrast:{label:'Contrast', desk:'#d9d9d9', page:'#ffffff', ink:'#000000', muted:'#333333', accent:'#9c2f00', hair:'rgba(0,0,0,.42)',     hairS:'rgba(0,0,0,.68)',    dim:'rgba(0,0,0,.22)',     sel:'rgba(156,47,0,.22)'}
};
function rgba(hex,a){
  const m=/^#?([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})$/i.exec(hex);
  return m?`rgba(${parseInt(m[1],16)},${parseInt(m[2],16)},${parseInt(m[3],16)},${a})`:hex;
}

/* ---------------- reading fonts (declared for the page AND the book iframes) ---------------- */
const FS=(fam,slug,w,style)=>`@font-face{font-family:'${fam}';font-style:${style||'normal'};font-weight:${w};font-display:swap;`+
  `src:url('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/fontsource/fonts/${slug}@latest/latin-${w}-${style||'normal'}.woff2') format('woff2');}`;
const FONT_FACES =
  FS('Literata','literata',400)+FS('Literata','literata',400,'italic')+FS('Literata','literata',600)+FS('Literata','literata',700)+
  FS('Atkinson Hyperlegible','atkinson-hyperlegible',400)+FS('Atkinson Hyperlegible','atkinson-hyperlegible',700)+
  FS('Lexend','lexend',400)+FS('Lexend','lexend',600)+FS('Lexend','lexend',700)+
  FS('OpenDyslexic','open-dyslexic',400)+FS('OpenDyslexic','open-dyslexic',700);
document.head.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<style>'+FONT_FACES+'</style>');

const FONTS = {
  literata:    {name:'Literata',            note:'Serif, tuned for e-reading',        stack:`'Literata',Georgia,'Times New Roman',serif`},
  atkinson:    {name:'Atkinson Hyperlegible',note:'Hyper-legible sans',               stack:`'Atkinson Hyperlegible','Lexend','Trebuchet MS',sans-serif`},
  lexend:      {name:'Lexend',              note:'Shaped for fluent reading',         stack:`'Lexend','Atkinson Hyperlegible',sans-serif`},
  opendyslexic:{name:'OpenDyslexic',        note:'Weighted, anti-rotation letterforms',stack:`'OpenDyslexic','Lexend','Atkinson Hyperlegible',sans-serif`}
};

/* ---------------- the fit engine ----------------
   Safari-Reader logic: type size follows the screen, the measure is capped
   so lines stay around 65–70 characters, and height keeps pages balanced. */
function fitInfo(){
  const m = settings.measure;
  const padX = clamp(innerWidth*0.05, 16, 48);
  const w = innerWidth - padX*2;
  const h = innerHeight - 150;
  const fs = Math.round( clamp(Math.min(w/m, 700/m, h/26), 17, 23) *10 )/10;
  return { fs, mw: Math.round(Math.min(m*fs, w)) };
}
function effSize(){ return settings.auto ? fitInfo().fs : settings.size; }
function coloText(){
  const es = effSize();
  const padX = clamp(innerWidth*0.05,16,48);
  const mw = Math.round(Math.min(settings.measure*es, innerWidth - padX*2));
  const adv = 0.5 + settings.letter/1000;              // letter-spacing widens glyphs
  const cpl = Math.round(mw/(es*adv));
  return `${settings.auto?'Fitted':'Manual'} · ${es.toFixed(1)}px · ${mw}px measure · ≈${cpl} characters a line`;
}
function applyFit(){
  const f = fitInfo(), es = effSize();
  const rs = document.documentElement.style;
  rs.setProperty('--fit-fs', es+'px');
  rs.setProperty('--fit-lh', settings.lineHeight);
  rs.setProperty('--fit-mw', f.mw+'px');
  rs.setProperty('--fit-font', (FONTS[settings.font]||FONTS.literata).stack);
  rs.setProperty('--mw', f.mw+'px');
  el('sizeOut').textContent = es.toFixed(1)+'px';
  el('msOut').textContent = settings.measure+'em';
  el('landingColophon').textContent = 'Set for this screen · '+coloText().replace(/^(Fitted|Manual) · /,'');
  el('colophon').textContent = coloText();
  el('sizeRange').value = es;
  setFill(el('sizeRange'));
  el('stage').classList.toggle('no-arrows', innerWidth - f.mw < 150);
}

/* ---------------- CSS injected into the book iframes ---------------- */
function buildCSS(){
  const t = THEMES[settings.theme]||THEMES.paper;
  const fs = effSize();
  const stack = (FONTS[settings.font]||FONTS.literata).stack;
  const j = settings.justify;
  return FONT_FACES + `
html{font-size:${fs}px !important;-webkit-text-size-adjust:100%;}
html,body{background:${t.page} !important;}
body{
  color:${t.ink} !important;
  font-family:${stack} !important;
  font-size:${fs}px !important;
  line-height:${settings.lineHeight} !important;
  letter-spacing:${(settings.letter/1000).toFixed(3)}em !important;
  word-spacing:${(settings.word/1000).toFixed(3)}em !important;
  text-align:${j?'justify':'left'} !important;
  margin:0 !important; padding:0 !important;
  -webkit-hyphens:auto; hyphens:auto; overflow-wrap:break-word;
}
p,li,dd,dt,blockquote,td,th,figcaption,div,section,article,span,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,a,strong,b,em,i,small,pre{
  letter-spacing:inherit !important; word-spacing:inherit !important; line-height:inherit !important;}
p,li,dd,dt,blockquote,td,th,figcaption,div,section,article,pre{font-size:1rem !important;}
h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6{font-family:${stack} !important;color:inherit !important;text-align:${j?'left':'inherit'} !important;}
p{margin:0 0 1.05em !important;text-indent:0 !important;orphans:2;widows:2;text-wrap:pretty;}
h1,h2,h3{margin:1.6em 0 .8em !important;}
blockquote{margin:1.3em 1.6em !important;padding-left:1.1em;border-left:2px solid ${t.hairS} !important;}
a{color:${t.accent} !important;text-underline-offset:.16em;}
img,svg,video,canvas{max-width:100% !important;height:auto !important;}
::selection{background:${t.sel};color:${t.ink};}
b.fio-b{font-weight:700;}
.epubjs-hl{background:${rgba(t.accent,.16)} !important;box-shadow:none !important;border-radius:2px;
  -webkit-box-decoration-break:clone;box-decoration-break:clone;}
`;
}
function eachContent(fn){
  const r = state.rendition; if(!r) return;
  let list=[]; try{ list = r.getContents ? (r.getContents()||[]) : []; }catch(e){}
  list.forEach(fn);
}
function refreshCSS(){
  const css = buildCSS();
  eachContent(c=>{
    const d=c.document; let s=d.getElementById('folio-dyn');
    if(!s){ s=d.createElement('style'); s.id='folio-dyn'; (d.head||d.documentElement).appendChild(s); }
    s.textContent = css;
  });
}

/* ---------------- app state ---------------- */
const state = { book:null, rendition:null, loading:false, posKey:null };
let tocItems = [];

/* ---------------- book lifecycle ---------------- */
function showLoader(msg){ el('loaderMsg').textContent=msg||'Typesetting…'; el('loader').classList.remove('hide'); state.t0=Date.now(); }
function hideLoader(){ const d=Date.now()-(state.t0||0); setTimeout(()=>el('loader').classList.add('hide'), Math.max(0,450-d)); }

async function closeBook(){
  ttsStop();
  try{ state.rendition && state.rendition.destroy(); }catch(e){}
  try{ state.book && state.book.destroy(); }catch(e){}
  state.rendition=null; state.book=null; el('viewer').innerHTML='';
}

async function openBuffer(buf){
  if(state.loading) return; state.loading=true;
  showLoader('Typesetting…');
  try{
    await closeBook();
    const book = ePub(buf);
    state.book = book;
    const md = (book.packaging && book.packaging.metadata) || {};
    const title = md.title || 'Untitled';
    el('bookTitle').textContent = title;
    state.posKey = 'folio-pos:'+title;

    const rendition = book.renderTo('viewer',{width:'100%',height:'100%',flow:'paginated',spread:'none',manager:'default'});
    state.rendition = rendition;
    rendition.hooks.content.register(onContent);
    rendition.on('rendered', onceHide);
    let hidden=false;
    function onceHide(){ if(hidden)return; hidden=true; hideLoader(); }
    setTimeout(onceHide, 9000);

    rendition.on('relocated', onRelocated);
    rendition.on('displayError', ()=>{ toast('That section could not be displayed'); });

    // reset chrome
    el('railFill').style.width='0%'; el('railKnob').style.left='0%';
    el('pct').textContent=''; el('chapter').textContent='';
    el('tocList').innerHTML='<li class="empty">Loading contents…</li>';

    let target = 0;
    try{ const saved=localStorage.getItem(state.posKey); if(saved) target=saved; }catch(e){}

    body.classList.add('state-reading');
    body.classList.remove('state-landing');
    applyFit();
    await rendition.display(target).catch(()=>rendition.display(0));

    buildTOC(book);
    if(!seenHint){ seenHint=true; toast('Swipe or tap the page edges to turn · tap the middle for controls'); }
    poke();
  }catch(err){
    console.error(err);
    body.classList.remove('state-reading');
    body.classList.add('state-landing');
    hideLoader();
    toast('Could not open that file — Folio reads DRM-free EPUBs');
  }finally{ state.loading=false; }
}
let seenHint=false;

function loadFile(file){
  if(!file) return;
  if(!/\.epub$/i.test(file.name) && file.type!=='application/epub+zip'){
    toast('That does not look like an EPUB file'); return;
  }
  showLoader('Opening…');
  const fr=new FileReader();
  fr.onload=()=>openBuffer(fr.result);
  fr.onerror=()=>{ hideLoader(); toast('The file could not be read'); };
  fr.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
}

/* ---------------- content hooks: styles, bionic, taps, keys ---------------- */
function onContent(contents){
  const d=contents.document;
  let s=d.getElementById('folio-dyn');
  if(!s){ s=d.createElement('style'); s.id='folio-dyn'; (d.head||d.documentElement).appendChild(s); }
  s.textContent=buildCSS();
  if(settings.bionic && d.body.dataset.fioBionic!=='1'){ bionifyDoc(d); d.body.dataset.fioBionic='1'; }
  wireContents(contents);
}
function wireContents(contents){
  const d=contents.document;
  d.addEventListener('click', e=>{
    const sel=d.getSelection?d.getSelection():null;
    if(sel && sel.toString().length) return;                    // don't turn pages while selecting
    if(e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a')) return; // let links work
    const w=(d.documentElement&&d.documentElement.clientWidth)||1;
    const x=e.clientX/w;
    if(x<0.3) turn(-1);
    else if(x>0.7) turn(1);
    else if(matchMedia('(hover: none)').matches) toggleChrome();
  });
  d.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
}

/* ---------------- bionic reading ---------------- */
function bionifyDoc(doc){
  const walker=doc.createTreeWalker(doc.body,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT,{acceptNode(n){
    if(!n.nodeValue||!n.nodeValue.trim())return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;
    const p=n.parentElement; if(!p)return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;
    const t=p.nodeName;
    if(t==='SCRIPT'||t==='STYLE'||t==='HEAD'||t==='TITLE')return NodeFilter.FILTER_REJECT;
    return NodeFilter.FILTER_ACCEPT;
  }});
  const nodes=[]; while(walker.nextNode())nodes.push(walker.currentNode);
  const WORD=/^([\P{L}\P{N}]*)([\p{L}\p{N}]+)([\s\S]*)$/u;
  nodes.forEach(node=>{
    const frag=doc.createDocumentFragment();
    node.nodeValue.split(/(\s+)/).forEach(tok=>{
      if(!tok)return;
      const m=tok.match(WORD);
      if(!m||m[2].length<2){frag.appendChild(doc.createTextNode(tok));return;}
      const n=Math.max(1,Math.round(m[2].length*0.42));
      if(m[1])frag.appendChild(doc.createTextNode(m[1]));
      const b=doc.createElement('b'); b.className='fio-b'; b.textContent=m[2].slice(0,n);
      frag.appendChild(b);
      if(m[2].length>n)frag.appendChild(doc.createTextNode(m[2].slice(n)));
      if(m[3])frag.appendChild(doc.createTextNode(m[3]));
    });
    node.parentNode.replaceChild(frag,node);
  });
}
function debionify(doc){
  doc.querySelectorAll('b.fio-b').forEach(b=>b.replaceWith(doc.createTextNode(b.textContent)));
  doc.body.normalize();
}
function setBionic(on){
  settings.bionic=on; el('swBionic').checked=on; save();
  eachContent(c=>{
    const d=c.document;
    if(on){ if(d.body.dataset.fioBionic!=='1'){ bionifyDoc(d); d.body.dataset.fioBionic='1'; } }
    else  { if(d.body.dataset.fioBionic==='1'){ debionify(d); d.body.dataset.fioBionic='0'; } }
  });
}

/* ---------------- relocation: progress, chapter, resume ---------------- */
function onRelocated(loc){
  if(!loc||!loc.start)return;
  const total=state.book.spine.length;
  const d=loc.start.displayed;
  const frac=clamp((loc.start.index+((d&&d.total)?(d.page-1)/d.total:0))/total,0,1);
  el('railFill').style.width=(frac*100)+'%';
  el('railKnob').style.left=(frac*100)+'%';
  el('pct').textContent=Math.round(frac*100)+'%';
  el('chapter').textContent=chapterFor(loc.start.href);
  markTocCurrent(loc.start.href);
  try{ localStorage.setItem(state.posKey, loc.start.cfi); }catch(e){}
}
function chapterFor(href){
  if(!href)return'';
  const base=x=>String(x).split('/').pop().split('#')[0];
  const it=tocItems.find(i=>base(i.href)===base(href));
  return it?it.label:'';
}
function turn(dir){
  if(!state.rendition)return;
  if(tts.active)ttsStop();
  dir>0?state.rendition.next():state.rendition.prev();
  poke();
}

/* ---------------- table of contents ---------------- */
async function buildTOC(book){
  try{
    const nav=await book.loaded.navigation;
    const out=[];
    (function flat(items,depth){
      (items||[]).forEach(it=>{
        out.push({label:String(it.label||'Untitled').trim(), href:it.href, depth});
        if(it.subitems&&it.subitems.length)flat(it.subitems,depth+1);
      });
    })(nav.toc,0);
    tocItems=out;
    const list=el('tocList'); list.innerHTML='';
    if(!out.length){ list.innerHTML='<li class="empty">This book has no table of contents.</li>'; return; }
    out.forEach((it,i)=>{
      const li=document.createElement('li');
      const b=document.createElement('button');
      b.textContent=it.label;
      b.style.paddingLeft=(20+it.depth*14)+'px';
      b.addEventListener('click',()=>{
        if(tts.active)ttsStop();
        closePanels();
        state.rendition&&state.rendition.display(it.href).catch(()=>{});
      });
      li.appendChild(b); list.appendChild(li);
    });
    markTocCurrent(state.rendition.currentLocation().start.href);
  }catch(e){
    el('tocList').innerHTML='<li class="empty">This book has no table of contents.</li>';
  }
}
function markTocCurrent(href){
  const base=x=>String(x||'').split('/').pop().split('#')[0];
  let idx=tocItems.findIndex(i=>base(i.href)===base(href));
  [...el('tocList').children].forEach((li,i)=>li.classList.toggle('cur',i===idx));
}

/* ---------------- read aloud (Web Speech) ---------------- */
const tts={active:false,paused:false,token:0};
let markedRange=null;
function chosenVoice(){
  if(!('speechSynthesis' in window))return null;
  const vs=speechSynthesis.getVoices(); if(!vs.length)return null;
  let v=vs.find(x=>x.voiceURI===settings.voice);
  if(!v){
    const lang=(((state.book||{}).packaging||{}).metadata||{}).language||'en';
    v=vs.find(x=>x.lang&&x.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(String(lang).slice(0,2).toLowerCase()));
  }
  return v||vs[0];
}
function speak(text){ return new Promise(res=>{
  const u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
  const v=chosenVoice(); if(v)u.voice=v;
  u.rate=settings.rate; u.pitch=1;
  let done=false; const fin=()=>{ if(!done){done=true;res();} };
  u.onend=fin; u.onerror=fin;
  speechSynthesis.speak(u);
});}
function splitSentences(t){
  const parts=t.split(/([.!?…]+)/), out=[]; let s='';
  for(let i=0;i<parts.length;i+=2){
    s+=parts[i];
    if(parts[i+1]){ s+=parts[i+1]; out.push(s.trim()); s=''; }
  }
  if(s.trim())out.push(s.trim());
  return out.filter(Boolean);
}
async function pageText(){
  const loc=state.rendition.currentLocation();
  if(!loc||!loc.start||!loc.end)return null;
  const cr=loc.start.cfi+','+loc.end.cfi;
  try{
    const range=await state.book.getRange(cr);
    const txt=(range&&range.toString?range.toString():'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    return txt?{txt,cr}:{err:true};
  }catch(e){ return {err:true}; }
}
function markPage(cr){ unmarkPage(); try{ state.rendition.annotations.add('highlight',cr,{},()=>{}); markedRange=cr; }catch(e){} }
function unmarkPage(){ if(markedRange&&state.rendition){ try{state.rendition.annotations.remove(markedRange,'highlight');}catch(e){} } markedRange=null; }
function nextRelocated(){ return new Promise(res=>{
  const r=state.rendition; if(!r)return res(null);
  const h=loc=>{ r.off('relocated',h); res(loc); };
  r.on('relocated',h); setTimeout(()=>{ r.off('relocated',h); res(null); },4000);
});}
function isLastPage(loc){
  if(!loc||!loc.start)return false;
  if(loc.start.index!==state.book.spine.length-1)return false;
  const d=loc.start.displayed; return !d||d.page>=d.total;
}
function syncSpeakBtn(){
  el('btnSpeak').classList.toggle('on',tts.active);
  el('btnSpeak').classList.toggle('live',tts.active&&!tts.paused);
}
async function speakToggle(){
  if(!state.rendition)return;
  if(!('speechSynthesis' in window)){ toast('Speech synthesis is not available in this browser'); return; }
  if(tts.active){
    if(speechSynthesis.paused){ speechSynthesis.resume(); tts.paused=false; }
    else if(speechSynthesis.speaking){ speechSynthesis.pause(); tts.paused=true; }
    syncSpeakBtn(); return;
  }
  if(!speechSynthesis.getVoices().length){
    await new Promise(r=>{ let n=0; const t=setInterval(()=>{ if(speechSynthesis.getVoices().length||++n>20){clearInterval(t);r();} },100); });
  }
  tts.active=true; tts.paused=false; syncSpeakBtn();
  const my=++tts.token;
  let guard=0;
  while(tts.token===my && tts.active && guard++<999){
    const p=await pageText();
    if(!p||p.err){ toast('Could not read this page aloud'); break; }
    markPage(p.cr);
    for(const s of splitSentences(p.txt)){
      if(tts.token!==my||!tts.active)break;
      await speak(s);
    }
    unmarkPage();
    if(tts.token!==my||!tts.active)break;
    const before=state.rendition.currentLocation();
    if(isLastPage(before)){ toast('The end.'); break; }
    state.rendition.next();
    const loc=await nextRelocated();
    if(!loc||!loc.start||(before&&loc.start.cfi===before.start.cfi))break;
  }
  if(tts.token===my){ tts.active=false; tts.paused=false; syncSpeakBtn(); }
}
function ttsStop(){ tts.token++; tts.active=false; tts.paused=false;
  if('speechSynthesis' in window)speechSynthesis.cancel(); unmarkPage(); syncSpeakBtn(); }

/* ---------------- reading guide ---------------- */
function setGuide(on){
  settings.guide=on; el('swGuide').checked=on; save();
  body.classList.toggle('guide-on',on);
  if(on){ toast('Reading guide on — it follows your pointer'); moveGuide(innerHeight/2); }
}
function moveGuide(y){ el('guide').style.top=(y-el('guide').offsetHeight/2)+'px'; }
addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{ if(settings.guide)moveGuide(e.clientY); },{passive:true});
addEventListener('touchmove',e=>{ if(settings.guide&&e.touches[0])moveGuide(e.touches[0].clientY); },{passive:true});

/* ---------------- chrome auto-hide ---------------- */
let hideTimer=null;
function poke(){
  if(!body.classList.contains('state-reading'))return;
  body.classList.remove('chrome-hidden');
  clearTimeout(hideTimer);
  hideTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
    if(body.classList.contains('state-reading')&&!body.classList.contains('sheet-open')&&!body.classList.contains('toc-open'))
      body.classList.add('chrome-hidden');
  },3400);
}
function toggleChrome(){ body.classList.toggle('chrome-hidden'); clearTimeout(hideTimer); }
['pointermove','pointerdown'].forEach(ev=>addEventListener(ev,()=>poke(),{passive:true}));

/* ---------------- panels ---------------- */
function toggleSheet(){ body.classList.toggle('sheet-open'); syncScrim(); poke(); }
function toggleToc(){ body.classList.toggle('toc-open'); syncScrim(); poke(); }
function closePanels(){ body.classList.remove('sheet-open'); body.classList.remove('toc-open'); syncScrim(); }
function syncScrim(){
  const s=body.classList.contains('toc-open')||(matchMedia('(max-width:760px)').matches&&body.classList.contains('sheet-open'));
  el('scrim').classList.toggle('show',s);
}
el('scrim').addEventListener('click',closePanels);

/* ---------------- toast ---------------- */
let toastT=null;
function toast(msg){
  const t=el('toast'); t.textContent=msg; t.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(toastT); toastT=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),2800);
}

/* ---------------- keyboard ---------------- */
function onKey(e){
  if(e.defaultPrevented||e.metaKey||e.ctrlKey||e.altKey)return;
  const tag=(e.target&&e.target.tagName)||'';
  if(/INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA/.test(tag))return;
  if(e.key==='Escape'){ closePanels(); return; }
  if(!state.rendition)return;
  switch(e.key){
    case 'ArrowRight': case 'PageDown': turn(1); e.preventDefault(); break;
    case 'ArrowLeft':  case 'PageUp':   turn(-1); e.preventDefault(); break;
    case ' ': turn(1); e.preventDefault(); break;
    case 't': toggleToc(); break;
    case 's': toggleSheet(); break;
    case 'g': setGuide(!settings.guide); break;
    case 'b': setBionic(!settings.bionic); toast(settings.bionic?'Bionic reading on':'Bionic reading off'); break;
    case 'p': speakToggle(); break;
  }
}
addEventListener('keydown',onKey);

/* ---------------- relayout ---------------- */
function relayout(){
  applyFit(); refreshCSS();
  if(state.rendition){
    const w=el('wrap');
    try{ state.rendition.resize(w.clientWidth,w.clientHeight); }catch(e){}
  }
}
let rzT=null;
addEventListener('resize',()=>{ clearTimeout(rzT); rzT=setTimeout(relayout,160); });

/* ---------------- theme ---------------- */
function applyTheme(){
  const t=THEMES[settings.theme]||THEMES.paper;
  const rs=document.documentElement.style;
  rs.setProperty('--desk',t.desk); rs.setProperty('--page',t.page);
  rs.setProperty('--ink',t.ink);   rs.setProperty('--muted',t.muted);
  rs.setProperty('--accent',t.accent); rs.setProperty('--hair',t.hair);
  rs.setProperty('--hairS',t.hairS);   rs.setProperty('--dim',t.dim);
  rs.setProperty('--sel',t.sel);
  document.querySelectorAll('#themeRow .t-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('sel',b.dataset.theme===settings.theme));
}

/* ---------------- settings UI ---------------- */
function setFill(input){
  const p=(input.value-input.min)/(input.max-input.min)*100;
  input.style.setProperty('--fill',p+'%');
}
function buildFontGrid(){
  const g=el('fontGrid'); g.innerHTML='';
  Object.entries(FONTS).forEach(([k,f])=>{
    const b=document.createElement('button');
    b.className='f-btn'; b.dataset.font=k;
    b.innerHTML=`<span class="f-name" style="font-family:${f.stack}">${f.name}</span><span class="f-note">${f.note}</span>`;
    b.addEventListener('click',()=>{ settings.font=k; save(); syncFontSel(); relayout(); });
    g.appendChild(b);
  });
  syncFontSel();
}
function syncFontSel(){
  document.querySelectorAll('#fontGrid .f-btn').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('sel',b.dataset.font===settings.font));
}
function buildThemeRow(){
  const r=el('themeRow'); r.innerHTML='';
  Object.entries(THEMES).forEach(([k,t])=>{
    const b=document.createElement('button');
    b.className='t-btn'; b.dataset.theme=k;
    b.innerHTML=`<i style="background:${t.page};box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 3px ${t.page},inset 0 0 0 20px ${t.ink}"></i>${t.label}`;
    b.addEventListener('click',()=>{ settings.theme=k; save(); applyTheme(); relayout(); });
    r.appendChild(b);
  });
}
function syncSize(){
  el('sizeRange').disabled=settings.auto;
  el('sizeAuto').classList.toggle('on',settings.auto);
  applyFit();
}
function nudgeSize(d){
  if(settings.auto)settings.auto=false;
  settings.size=clamp(Math.round(((settings.auto?fitInfo().fs:settings.size)+d)*10)/10,15,28);
  save(); syncSize(); relayout();
}
function buildVoices(){
  if(!('speechSynthesis' in window))return;
  const sel=el('voiceSel'); sel.innerHTML='';
  const auto=document.createElement('option');
  auto.value=''; auto.textContent='Automatic (match the book)';
  sel.appendChild(auto);
  speechSynthesis.getVoices().forEach(v=>{
    const o=document.createElement('option');
    o.value=v.voiceURI; o.textContent=`${v.name} (${v.lang})`;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });
  sel.value=settings.voice||'';
  if(sel.value!==(settings.voice||''))sel.value='';
}
function syncControls(){
  syncFontSel(); applyTheme();
  el('swBionic').checked=settings.bionic;
  el('swGuide').checked=settings.guide;
  body.classList.toggle('guide-on',settings.guide);
  el('swJustify').checked=settings.justify;
  el('lhRange').value=settings.lineHeight; el('lhOut').textContent=(+settings.lineHeight).toFixed(2);
  el('lsRange').value=settings.letter;     el('lsOut').textContent=(settings.letter/1000).toFixed(3)+'em';
  el('wsRange').value=settings.word;       el('wsOut').textContent=(settings.word/1000).toFixed(3)+'em';
  el('msRange').value=settings.measure;
  el('rateRange').value=settings.rate;     el('rateOut').textContent='×'+(+settings.rate).toFixed(2);
  ['lhRange','lsRange','wsRange','msRange','rateRange'].forEach(id=>setFill(el(id)));
  syncSize(); buildVoices();
}

el('rail').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  if(!state.rendition)return;
  const r=el('rail').getBoundingClientRect();
  const f=clamp((e.clientX-r.left)/r.width,0,1);
  const total=state.book.spine.length;
  const sec=state.book.spine.get(clamp(Math.floor(f*total),0,total-1));
  if(sec){ if(tts.active)ttsStop(); state.rendition.display(sec.href).catch(()=>{}); }
});

/* ---------------- drag & drop ---------------- */
let depth=0;
const hasFiles=e=>e.dataTransfer&&[...e.dataTransfer.types].includes('Files');
addEventListener('dragenter',e=>{ if(hasFiles(e)){ e.preventDefault(); if(++depth===1)body.classList.add('dropping'); }});
addEventListener('dragover',e=>{ if(hasFiles(e))e.preventDefault(); });
addEventListener('dragleave',e=>{ if(hasFiles(e)&&--depth<=0){depth=0;body.classList.remove('dropping');} });
addEventListener('drop',e=>{
  if(!hasFiles(e))return;
  e.preventDefault(); depth=0; body.classList.remove('dropping');
  loadFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
});

<div class="tp-rule"></div>
<p>An abridgement in three chapters,<br/>set by Folio as a working specimen.</p>
<p>London · 1895</p></div>`));

    zip.file('OEBPS/ch1.xhtml',XHTML('I — The Fourth Dimension',`
<h2>I — The Fourth Dimension</h2>
<p>The Time Traveller (for so it will be convenient to speak of him) was expounding a recondite matter to us. His grey eyes shone and twinkled, and his usually pale face was flushed and animated. The fire burned brightly, and the soft radiance of the incandescent lights in the lilies of silver caught the bubbles that flashed and passed in our glasses.</p>
<p>Our chairs, being his patents, embraced and caressed us rather than submitted to be sat upon; and there was that luxurious after-dinner atmosphere, when thought runs gracefully free of the trammels of precision. And slowly and patiently, the Time Traveller laid his before us.</p>
<p>You must follow me carefully, he said. I shall have to dispute one or two points that are almost universally accepted. The geometry, for instance, which they have taught you at school is founded on a misconception. Is not that rather a large thing to expect us to begin upon? said Filby, an argumentative person with red hair. I do not mean to ask you to accept anything without reasonable ground for belief, the Time Traveller replied, and soon you will easily grant me the point I ask.</p>
<blockquote><p>Clearly, the Time Traveller proceeded, any real body must have extension in four directions: it must have Length, Breadth, Thickness, and Duration. There are really four dimensions, three which we call the three planes of Space, and a fourth, Time. There is, however, a tendency to draw an unreal distinction between the former three dimensions and the latter, because it happens that our consciousness moves intermittently in one direction along the latter, from the beginning of our existence to the end.</p></blockquote>
<p>But, said the Medical Man, staring hard at a poker in the fire, a thing cannot be in different parts of Space at the same moment. Scientific people, the Time Traveller remarked, with a glance at the fire, know very well that Time is only a kind of Space. Here is a portrait of a man at eight years old, another at fifteen, another at seventeen, another at twenty-three. All these are evidently sections, as it were, Three-Dimensional representations of his Four-Dimensioned being, which is a fixed and unalterable thing.</p>
<p>There was a murmur of dissent. Filby pronounced the notion fanciful and unbusinesslike, and the Very Young Man demanded, above the clink of the glasses, why one should not move about in Time as freely as one moved about in Space. The Time Traveller only smiled, and tapped his forefinger on the arm of his chair.</p>
<p>That, he said, is the beginning. Tonight I will show you a machine — and if it does what I believe it can do, none of us will sleep very soundly again.</p>`));

    zip.file('OEBPS/ch2.xhtml',XHTML('II — The Model Machine',`
<h2>II — The Model Machine</h2>
<p>The next Thursday we came again: the Psychologist, the Medical Man, Filby, the Very Young Man, and myself. We found the Time Traveller a little battered and dusty about the elbows, and a little hollow in the cheek. I am dying to show you something, he said, and led us down the long draughty corridor to the laboratory.</p>
<p>On the table lay a little mechanism of glittering metal, scarcely larger than a small clock, very delicately made. There was ivory in it, and some transparent crystalline substance. This little thing, said the Time Traveller, resting his elbows on the table and pressing his hands together above it, is a model of a machine that will travel through Time.</p>
<p>This lever here, he went on, sends the thing gliding into the future. This other sends it into the past. I will press one of them, and the little machine will vanish from this room, from this moment, into whatever lies ahead of it. The Psychologist, at a sign from our host, put out his finger and pressed the gleaming lever.</p>
<p>There was a breath of wind, a flicker of the lamplight, a little turning of the flames in the grate — and where the model had stood there was nothing at all. We looked at the bare table. Somebody gasped. The Psychologist recovered from his stupor, and suddenly looked under the table.</p>
<p>Are you in earnest? said the Medical Man. Is it a trick — a conjuring trick? That, said the Time Traveller, is no trick. The little machine is at this moment travelling onward, year by year, in the middle of that room. It is among the drifting smoke of the lamp, and it is a year away, and it is a thousand years away, all at once.</p>
<p>We stood around the empty place a long time, no one speaking. Outside, the evening deepened; and I remember thinking that somewhere down the far slope of Time that small bright thing was still falling, and would be falling still when every one of us was gone.</p>
<p>The full-sized machine, said the Time Traveller at last, blowing out the lamp, stands in the laboratory. Come back on Thursday — and bring your nerve with you.</p>`));

    zip.file('OEBPS/ch3.xhtml',XHTML('III — The Sensation of a Leap',`
<h2>III — The Sensation of a Leap</h2>
<p>When we came again the next Thursday, the laboratory stood empty, and the lamp was cold. And then, in the doorway: haggard and pale, with a dirty bandage on his ankle and his clothes dusty and disordered, the Time Traveller himself came in — returned from wherever the machine had taken him.</p>
<p>I started the machine that morning, he said, when we had drawn up to the fire. I pressed the lever for the future. And I am afraid I cannot convey the peculiar sensations of travelling through Time. It is a headlong fall, a ghastly flipping of days and nights. The laboratory grew misty; Mrs. Watchett, my housekeeper, crossed the room like a rocket; and then the night came down like the shutting of a black lid.</p>
<blockquote><p>As I went on, night followed day like the flipping of a black and white page. The dim outline of the laboratory vanished. I saw the sun spin across the sky in rings of light; I saw the sky take on a wonderful tint of dawn and dusk together, and the stars beat in and out among them. The landscape rose and fell like the tide; the trees grew and changed and passed like breath; and building after building, age after age, flashed into being and was gone.</p></blockquote>
<p>I was afraid to stop. And then, all at once, the easing of that terrific pace told me I must. I brought the machine to rest — and the sun stood still in a sky of exquisite sunset, and before me, washed in that gentler light, lay a great and beautiful architecture, and a small, graceful people gathered silently to look at me, as quiet as the statues they lived among.</p>
<p>Of what I found there — of the two peoples of that far afternoon of the world, the childlike Eloi above the ground and the pale Morlocks below it — of the dark wells, and the fire I carried into the under-world — I will tell you everything, if you will bear with me. I am tired, and limping, and hungry; but I have by me two strange white flowers, shrunken and brown now, that grew beside the way, to remind me of that other and happier morning.</p>
<p>He paused. The fire had burnt low, and the rest of us sat very still, our glasses forgotten. Somewhere in the laboratory, faint in the draught, something ticked — and for the first time that evening, I believed him.</p>`));

    zip.file('OEBPS/nav.xhtml',`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
```

**How the auto-fit works (the Safari-Reader part).** A small fit engine reads the real viewport and solves for a base size: `clamp(17px, min(column ÷ measure, 700px ÷ measure, height ÷ 26), 23px)`. In practice that gives ~17px on a phone, ~20.5px on an iPad, ~20.6px on desktop — always capped so the measure never exceeds ~700px, which lands lines in the 65–70 character band Safari targets. Leading defaults to 1.65, paragraphs get clean rhythm and `text-wrap: pretty`, hyphenation is on, and everything re-fits live on resize/rotation. The colophon line (on the landing page and at the foot of the typography sheet) shows the math it computed for *your* screen: size, measure, and estimated characters per line.

**The dyslexia toolkit** (all of it helps any reader): OpenDyslexic, Atkinson Hyperlegible, and Lexend alongside Literata; bionic reading (bolded word-starts, applied safely inside the book's iframe via a `TreeWalker`); letter- and word-spacing sliders; line-height and line-length control; a soft-edged reading guide that tracks your pointer and dims everything off-line; and read-aloud via the Web Speech API — it highlights the current page, reads sentence by sentence, and turns pages by itself until you pause or stop.

**Other details:** page turns work by swipe, tap-zones (30% edges), click, arrow keys, or Space; the chrome auto-hides after ~3.5s and returns on movement; progress rail, TOC drawer, four paper themes (including a true high-contrast mode); settings and reading positions persist in `localStorage`. Everything runs locally — no server, no keys. Two honest limits: it opens DRM-free EPUBs only, and TTS quality depends on the voices your OS provides.

Page turn animation in spread mode:

snapshotPage(): captures the whole spread iframe (both pages) as one snapshot. For slide: whole spread slides - looks fine (like flipping a full sheet). For paper flip: flipping the whole spread as one leaf is wrong physically — should flip only the outer leaf (right page forward / left page backward) while the other page stays, revealing the under-pages. Proper book flip: forward turn: right page rotates left around gutter; beneath it shows next right page... but with epub.js single-iframe spread, the "next" content isn't rendered beneath until rendition.next() happens.
This is getting complex. Simpler credible approach for spread flip: animate only the leading half (right half for forward) as the leaf; the remaining half (left page) stays static; underneath (the incoming spread) is hidden until animation completes, then swap. During the flip, under the leaf we show the book's under-page color (paper) — since real under content can't be known pre-advance. Actually we CAN know: snapshot current spread, then call rendition.next() immediately (like current design), then snapshot again? The new spread's iframe — after next() resolves, take second snapshot of the NEW spread and place it underneath, then flip the old right-half leaf over it. That's the deluxe version! Feasible: after raw() advance, wait for 'relocated' then a paint delay, snapshot new iframe, position it under, build leaf from old snapshot's right (or left) half via a wrapper with overflow hidden and negative offset.

Complexity is high but let's attempt:

playTurnFx spread paper flip:

part = snapshotPage() (full spread).
mount snap (clone) over viewer.
raw() advance → wait relocated (or small delay) → part2 = snapshotPage() (new spread) → mount part2 as underlay BEFORE flipping.
Leaf: wrapper div positioned over the half being flipped: for forward: right half: left = r.left + r.width/2, width r.width/2. Inside wrapper, inner div width = r.width (full spread) with left offset -(r.width/2) containing the old snapshot iframe → shows old right page. Backface: rotate the leaf; back face shows new spread's left half? Physically when page flips, its back side becomes visible showing... in real book, the back of current right page is the next-left page. We have part2 (new spread) — its left half IS the back of the flipped page! So back face = part2's left half.
So: front face = old right half; back face = new left half (pre-rotated 180). As leaf rotates from 0 → -180 (forward), front (old right) shows until 90, then back (new left) shows — exactly like a real book!! And underneath: left static old page (until covered?) — hmm, during forward flip, the left page stays old-left until flip completes, then it becomes new-left (which equals back face content — consistent!). The revealed right under the leaf = new-right (part2's right half). So underlay = part2 full spread placed beneath leaf. Left half: we keep old-left visible (the mounted old snapshot full spread, whose right half is covered by leaf) and at end swap old snapshot → remove, revealing part2 fully. The back face (new-left) rotating in will visually match the left half swap at 180°... At completion: leaf at -180° lying on left side showing new-left content; then we remove both leaf and old snapshot, leaving part2 fully visible. new-left == leaf back content, so seamless!

Wait, but during rotation the leaf covers area from center to left as it rotates — it sweeps over the old-left page region. Beneath leaf during sweep should be new-left (part2), but we have old-left there until removal at end. At -180 the leaf occupies exactly left half, showing new-left; then we remove leaf AND old snapshot simultaneously → part2 visible. The only imperfection: during the last 90° of sweep, under the leaf is old-left but leaf is opaque so not visible. At end, leaf back == new-left == part2 left → pixel identical swap.

For backward turn: mirror — leaf = old left half rotates 0→+180 around right edge (gutter), front = old-left, back = new-right (part2's right half), underlay part2.

This is the deluxe real-book flip. Need waiting for relocated: currently playTurnFx calls raw() and animates immediately; for spread flip we must await relocation before snapshotting part2. But waiting for relocated may take time; we can start leaf animation after underlay ready. Sequence: mount old snap; raw(); await relocated (with 1200ms timeout fallback); await part2 mount (paint delay ~120ms or load event); then build leaf + underlay (underlay inserted BELOW leaf z-index but above old snap: order: old snap z 61? underlay z 60, leaf z 62). Then animate.

Snapshot part2 = current iframe doc → new spread. Good.

function createRendition(){

setSpread(on):

js

async function applySpread(){
  const want=effectiveSpread();
  if(state.spreadOn===undefined) state.spreadOn=want;
  if(want===state.spreadOn || !state.rendition) {state.spreadOn=want; body.classList.toggle('spread',want); return;}
  if(state.turning){ setTimeout(applySpread, 700); return; } // retry after turn
  const cfi = safeCFI();
  state.spreadOn=want;
  body.classList.toggle('spread',want);
  try{ state.rendition.destroy(); }catch(e){}
  state.rendition=null;
  createRendition();
  applyFit(); // sizes
  if(cfi) await state.rendition.display(cfi).catch(()=>state.rendition.display(0));
  refreshCSS()? content hook does it.
}

spreadInfo/fit integration:

js

function spreadMetrics(){
  const padX=clamp(innerWidth*0.05,16,48);
  const availW=innerWidth-padX*2;
  const gutter=56;
  const pageMax=Math.min((availW-gutter)/2, 620);
  const fs=clamp(Math.min(pageMax/settings.measure, 700/settings.measure, (innerHeight-150)/26),14,23);
  const feasible = pageMax/settings.measure >= 15;
  const pageW=Math.round(Math.min(pageMax, fs*settings.measure));
  return {feasible, fs:Math.round(fs*10)/10, pageW, mw:pageW*2+gutter};
}
effectiveSpread(): if(state.book && (settings.spread==='on' || settings.spread==='auto') && spreadMetrics().feasible && innerWidth> innerHeight*? (allow landscape phones? width>=~1080 anyway)) → true.

applySpread full:

js

let spreadBusy=false;
async function applySpread(){
  const want=effectiveSpread();
  const changed=(want!==state.spreadOn);
  state.spreadOn=want;
  body.classList.toggle('spread',want);
  syncSpreadUI?.();
  if(!changed||!state.rendition||spreadBusy) return;
  spreadBusy=true; state.recreating=true;
  try{
    if(state.turning){ state.spreadPending=true; return; }
    let cfi=null; try{const l=state.rendition.currentLocation(); cfi=l&&l.start?l.start.cfi:null;}catch(e){}
    try{state.rendition.destroy();}catch(e){}
    state.rendition=null;
    createRendition({});
    if(cfi){ await state.rendition.display(cfi).catch(async()=>{ await state.rendition.display(0); }); }
    refreshCSS();
  } finally { spreadBusy=false; state.recreating=false;
    if(state.spreadPending){ state.spreadPending=false; applySpread(); } }
}
turning pending: if user resized mid-turn. Also after playTurnFx finally: if(state.spreadPending){state.spreadPending=false; applySpread();}

createRendition:

js

function createRendition(opts={}){
  const spreadOn=effectiveSpread();
  const r=state.book.renderTo('viewer',{width:'100%',height:'100%',flow:'paginated',spread:spreadOn?'always':'none',manager:'default'});
  r.hooks.content.register(onContent);
  r.on('relocated',onRelocated);
  r.on('displayError',()=>toast('That section could not be displayed'));
  if(opts.first){
    let hidden=false;
    const onceHide=()=>{if(hidden)return;hidden=true;hideLoader();};
    r.on('rendered',onceHide);
    setTimeout(onceHide,9000);
  }
  state.rendition=r;
  return r;
}
openBuffer previously had onceHide & listeners inline — now via createRendition.

One more: effectiveSpread():

js

function effectiveSpread(){
  if(!state.book) return false;
  if(state.turning) return !!state.spreadOn; // don't flip mid-turn decision
  const sm=spreadMetrics();
  if(settings.spread==='on') return sm.feasible;
  if(settings.spread==='auto') return sm.feasible && innerWidth>=1024;
  return false;
}
feasible already implies width; keep explicit 1024 for auto to avoid tablets portrait borderline? feasible needs ~1100 anyway; drop extra check — simpler: auto → sm.feasible.

spreadMetrics feasible threshold: pageMax/measure>=15. With measure 34: pageMax≥510 → availW≥ 5102+56=1076 +padX2(≈ up to 96) → ~1150. For measure 28 (min): 448*2+56=952+pad → ~1010. So wide screens/tablets landscape yes; phones no. Good.

Also max page 620 → fs cap via 700/measure? spreadMetrics fs = clamp(min(pageMax/m, 700/m? no—use pageMax/m and h/26),14,23) then feasible check separately. Let's finalize:

js

function spreadMetrics(){
  const padX=clamp(innerWidth*0.05,16,48);
  const gutter=Math.round(clamp(innerWidth*0.03,40,72));
  const availW=innerWidth-padX*2;
  const pageMax=Math.min((availW-gutter)/2,620);
  const h=innerHeight-150;
  const fsRaw=Math.min(pageMax/settings.measure,h/26);
  const fs=Math.round(clamp(fsRaw,14,22)*10)/10;
  const feasible=fsRaw>=15;
  const pageW=Math.round(Math.min(pageMax,fs*settings.measure));
  return {feasible,fs,pageW,gutter,mw:pageW*2+gutter};
}
gutter var could also feed #gutter width via CSS var --gut: set in applyFit when spread: rs.setProperty('--gut',sm.gutter+'px'); CSS gutter width uses var(--gut,64px).

Two-page spread is in. Since epub.js renders one iframe per view, the spread is done the native way — the renderer is created with `spread:'always'` and sized to the full open book — and the "bookholder" feel comes from a dressing layer on top: a shaded gutter down the middle, inset page-edge lines, a deep drop shadow, rounded outer corners, and a small wooden stand (ledge + back support) under the book. The layout engine gained a second solver: in spread mode it sizes each page so a line still holds ~your chosen character count, and it automatically falls back to one page when the window is too narrow (Auto / Two pages / One page, plus a `D` shortcut and a topbar toggle).

The paper flip was also rebuilt for spreads — it's now a *real* book turn: only the leading page lifts off the gutter, its back side is the next spread's opposite page (snapshotted after the renderer advances underneath), so the reveal is physically correct instead of flipping the whole open book as one slab.

Here's the complete regenerated file:

```html
  <footer class="landing-foot">Auto-fit typography <i>·</i> Two-page spread <i>·</i> Page-flip &amp; slide <i>·</i> Four typefaces <i>·</i> Bionic reading <i>·</i> Read-aloud</footer>
</div>
```
